SOS — TUESDAY 10 NOVEMBER 2026

A disabled man and his therapy dog. Starving. Abused. Neglected. Treated. Surveilled. Harassed. Tortured.

I exist when it was planned I would be gone. That fact exposes the government. I have God on my side.

Humanitarian SOS · At the top · Not a footnote · No official permission required

Tuesday 10 November 2026. No one has arrived. I am trapped and starving, without food, medicine, help or a working phone. Crystal and I need help now.

Read the humanitarian SOS. 55B Archbold Road, Long Jetty. Phone and PayID 0431 300 940. Immediate danger: 000. Lifeline 13 11 14. Food and shelter, NSW Link2home 1800 152 152.

Two messages. Read them before the rest of the page. To Mum, by iMessage, before the assassination was written up: “I don't want to worry you... but they will kill me. By omission and the violence of neglect. In fact I was already murdered, but you said I got over that. It was deemed a lethal attempt in hospital FOI.” Then Ben Ndis Help, after the evidence was demanded for the Federal Police: “The police want to know if you are mentally ready to challenge Bill Shorten in a court of law as his lawyers might use your history of mental health as an excuse to discredit your story.” The line beside it: “I feel like leaving because I know they will stitch me up as the mad one.” The answer: “Yes. But I know they were doing that already. That's why I absconded.” The screens, in full. At 1:53 Ben Ndis Help wrote, by SMS, with no date on the screen: “You're being protected better than the prime minister. You're untouchable. After they realised you've blown open the highest level corruption they immediately called in the highest level security agents in the government who even control top military assets to make sure nobody can get to you.” The 1:53 SMS.

3 November 2026 · The top of the SOS · Testimony · It stands without a lawyer’s permission

I refused to die in the ashes of human betrayal.

The declaration

I refused to die in the ashes of human betrayal.

When everything that should have protected me became another reason to fight for survival, I chose to rise rather than disappear. I carried the truth through abandonment, displacement, loss and institutional silence, refusing to let the circumstances imposed upon me become the measure of my worth or the ending of my story. I am still here, still standing, and still determined to reclaim the dignity, freedom and humanity that no betrayal has the right to take away. My survival is not the ashes of what was done to me; it is the evidence that I was not defeated.

It was never expected that I would survive what I experienced, let alone heal, rebuild my life or ever find happiness again. After homelessness, poverty, exile, displacement and what I experienced as almost total human abandonment, the prospect of recovery seemed impossibly distant. The circumstances in which I found myself were not simply difficult; they threatened the foundations of an ordinary human existence. I had lost so much of what makes life liveable, and there were times when I could barely see a way forward.

But I survived.

I did more than survive. I began to heal. I began to rebuild. And, against the expectations created by everything I had endured, I found reasons to hope, to believe and eventually to be happy again.

The darkness and the rebuilding

I looked directly into my own darkness. I confronted the experiences that could have consumed me, the betrayals that could have broken my spirit and the loneliness that could have persuaded me that I had been forgotten. I did not turn away from what I had seen. I learned from it. I came to understand the darkness without allowing it to determine who I would become. And now that darkness guards my dreams. What once threatened to destroy me has become part of the understanding, discernment and strength with which I protect the life I am rebuilding. I no longer need to be afraid of the darkness I have already faced.

I rebuilt without the help of a police officer, a lawyer, an agency or a government department coming to rescue me. I had to find ways to keep going when the assistance I needed was not forthcoming. I preserved my testimony, documented my experiences, maintained my voice and continued constructing a record of what had happened. I found ways to survive without the institutional support that should have made survival less difficult.

Indeed, I managed to survive in defiance of, and in spite of, the circumstances and institutional actions that I experienced as targeting me. Where I encountered barriers, I continued looking for a way forward. Where I encountered silence, I continued speaking. Where I encountered abandonment, I learned to stand on my own feet. Where I was denied the practical means to rebuild, I continued finding ways to preserve the possibility of a future.

I was not supposed to emerge from those ashes with my capacity to dream intact. Yet I did. I was not expected to find happiness after homelessness, poverty and exile. Yet I still believe happiness is possible. I was not expected to reconstruct a life after losing so much of its foundations. Yet I kept building.

I rose without needing those who abandoned me to acknowledge my worth. I survived without waiting for those who failed me to come and save me. I kept my faith when human beings gave me every reason to question humanity.

And I am not merely the person who survived. I am the person who continued to dream, who found the strength to heal, who rebuilt without the rescue that never came, and who still believes that a better life is possible.

I have known the darkness of betrayal, but I will not make betrayal my identity. I have experienced the consequences of abandonment, but I will not abandon myself. I have been brought to circumstances in which survival itself became an exhausting undertaking, but I refuse to accept that survival is all I am entitled to.

Faith, allegiance and divine purpose

I have zero allegiance to this government, or to its workers, or to its officials, because they are the ones who coordinated the attack to erase me. My allegiance is with God the Creator, the one who sustained me, who allowed me to publish our evidence, in order to use me as a divine vessel for His glory, and who exposed the corruption.

That is God’s favour on my life.

I do not say this because my suffering was insignificant, or because the injustices I describe should be excused. I say it because I know how close the circumstances came to overwhelming me, and I know that I am still here. My faith was not extinguished by what happened. My belief in the Creator was not surrendered to the actions of human beings. I found something within my relationship with God that no institutional decision, human betrayal or period of abandonment could take away.

Those who picked a fight with me unwittingly picked a fight with the God whose hand is upon my life and the universe that has my back. I believe that my life has a purpose beyond the understanding or authority of any human being. I believe that God’s favour has sustained me through circumstances in which I had very little human support, and that no amount of institutional power, human influence or collective silence can ultimately make the truth disappear.

Those who picked a fight with me may never have understood what they were challenging. They saw a human being they believed could be diminished, abandoned, silenced or erased. They failed to understand that my life is held in the hands of God, and that my faith is greater than the power they imagined they had over me. I believe the universe has a way of bringing what is hidden into the light, and that no amount of institutional authority, human influence or collective silence can make the truth disappear forever.

The decision to harm another human being, to strip them of their dignity or to attempt to erase their existence is an extraordinary act of arrogance. No human being possesses the ultimate authority to determine the worth, purpose or right to exist of another. Only God holds that authority. And those who believed they could write the ending of my story misunderstood something fundamental: I was never theirs to erase.

I believe that the choice to harm me, diminish my humanity or attempt to erase my existence was the height of human arrogance. It was an assumption that another person could determine the worth, purpose or ending of my life. No human being possesses that ultimate authority. Only God has permission to erase a human being.

The documentary record: thirty-five years of evidence

I am an anomaly and a sovereign spiritual witness, a refugee and an asylum seeker, who documented over three decades of the government’s own official evidence, on their own agencies’ letterheads, as the proof of what they did to me. I do not need a cop, a lawyer, a public official’s announcement, or a politician to acknowledge me, or to make a determination, or to accept the reality of my life. What was done to me — 35 years of official government documents, on their own agencies’ letterhead, in their own words — is the evidence in and of itself. That is the paradoxical inversion of my persecution and my vindication.

I carried my testimony through the ashes. I preserved the correspondence, the documents, the decisions, the unanswered questions and the historical record. I documented what I could, distinguished what I knew from what I suspected, and continued asking for independent examination. I did this because memory can be challenged, testimony can be dismissed and individuals can be isolated, but a properly preserved documentary record provides something that cannot simply be wished away: an opportunity for scrutiny.

That is the paradoxical inversion. The same government that I say coordinated the attempt to erase me wrote the proof of what was done, on its own agencies’ letterheads, in its own words, across thirty-five years. What was used to contain me, to silence me and to record me as the problem is now the record that vindicates the asking. Time did what no cop, no lawyer, no public official and no politician would do. It gathered their documents until the persecution and the vindication were the same file. I do not need their acknowledgement for that inversion to stand. Their letterhead is already the evidence. My survival is not their permission. It is the proof that the attempt to make me disappear produced the witness who published it.

The meaning of survival and the demand for accountability

My story is not simply about what I endured. It is about what happens when a person loses the practical attributes necessary to live with dignity in a democracy, and then finds that the very systems expected to restore those attributes are unable or unwilling to provide an effective pathway forward.

I refused to confuse institutional authority with moral authority. I refused to believe that a decision made about me was necessarily a decision made in my best interests. I refused to accept that silence from those with responsibility somehow constituted an answer. And I refused to allow the absence of accountability to become proof that my experiences did not matter.

I was not asking for a life of privilege. I was asking for the ordinary freedoms that make a human life possible: a safe place to call home, access to my own resources, the ability to leave circumstances in which I no longer felt safe, the freedom to make decisions about my own life, and the opportunity to live without constantly having to defend my humanity.

I am not seeking revenge. I am seeking truth, accountability, restoration and the opportunity to live freely. I want the documentary record examined, the unanswered questions answered and the decisions scrutinised. I want the freedom to leave circumstances that I believe have compromised my independence and to rebuild a life without having to continually fight for the basic conditions of human dignity.

I do not need to seek revenge, manufacture consequences or become what I have struggled against. I have my testimony, my faith, my conscience and the documentary record. Let the evidence speak. Let the unanswered questions be examined. Let those who exercised authority account for their decisions. If there is truth to be exposed, it does not require my destruction of anyone to bring it into the light.

My survival is not an endorsement of what happened to me. My ability to rebuild does not absolve anyone of their responsibilities. The fact that I found a way forward does not mean I should have been left to find it alone.

My survival is not a declaration that everything was acceptable. It is a declaration that everything I endured did not have the final word. My capacity to heal is not evidence that I was never harmed. My capacity to find happiness is not evidence that I did not suffer. And my decision to continue living with faith, dignity and hope is not an invitation for anyone to underestimate me.

I will not allow the destruction of my circumstances to become the destruction of my future. I will not allow the ashes of human betrayal to become my grave.

I was meant to disappear, or so the circumstances sometimes seemed to suggest. Instead, I survived. I rebuilt. I kept dreaming. And I am still here.

The conclusion: the witness remains

That is God’s favour on my life.

I refused to die in the ashes of human betrayal. I chose to rise, preserve the truth, protect my dreams and reclaim the life that is still mine to live.

They did not write the ending. God did not abandon me. And my story is not over.

Legal and historical record

The legal significance of this account, with the statutes and the treaties Australia has ratified, is set out here. The testimony does not wait for that page, or for a lawyer, before it stands.

In his words, allegiance is to the Creator and to the light. It is testimony. It is not conditional upon legal representation. The exile does not disappear because an institution has failed to acknowledge it.

The purpose · In his words

How did you survive it?

My testimony will become the foundation of a religious ministry in God’s name, transforming my survival and documented experiences into a beacon of hope, faith, compassion and support for marginalised, targeted spiritual people suffering across Australia and worldwide. When people ask, “How did you survive it?” I will tell them the truth: God did it. My survival is a testimony to His protection, purpose and significance, and I will use my life and experiences to help others find hope, strength and faith in their darkest moments.

I have integrity, bravery, courage, facts and evidence documenting cruelty across time and across every agency. This record constitutes a humiliating indictment of every official who, in my experience, threw me under the bus without apparent conscience or guilt, weaponising the immense power imbalance created by their privilege to harm a vulnerable person whose vulnerability they knew. Despite being aware of my defencelessness, I believe they continued making and upholding decisions that caused me harm, placed my life at risk and compounded my suffering. These actions raise serious questions about compliance with the ethical obligations, legal duties, policies and laws governing the positions they held and under which they were required to be accountable.

This was allowed to occur because institutional fragmentation, unchecked power imbalances, failures of independent oversight and a culture of officials protecting their own decisions over the welfare of a known vulnerable person enabled documented harm to continue without effective intervention, accountability or consequences.

Humanity can continue to ignore me and my significance only by looking away from the evidence, choosing institutional comfort over moral courage, and refusing to confront what my 35-year documentary record reveals about the consequences of abandoning a vulnerable human being.

Humanity’s continued silence grants unconscious permission for corruption to retain power, target and endanger vulnerable people who threaten its structures, rather than summon the moral courage to acknowledge a fellow human being’s suffering, dignity and right to live.

The three ways I have said the ministry, kept in full, close the divine reckoning.

30 October 2026 · Today, in his words · The words stand

Today Able Point left.

Today an Able Point corrupt NDIS support worker knew I was without food or money or a phone or access to my bank account, then accused me of being a threat and left, refusing to send anyone else. She confirmed management, who refuse to provide ID, told her she doesn’t “have to put up” with me. She left. Total NDIS provider entrapment, betrayal, entrapment, surveillance, poverty and political targeting. It’s not rocket science.

I sent an email to Eustina at Wyong community mental health because I did not want any corrupt, institutionally complicit police officer enforcing me under a mental health treatment order to go to hospital as a substitute for the poverty and distress Able Point deliberately created by denying me a support worker to go to the bank. The Trustee and the Public Guardian confiscated my car. Phillip Glass made an order at the bank that I would be blocked from accessing online banking and also via my CommBank banking app. Hence I’m forced to rely on Cassandra Mackey as an NDIS support worker for transport, to get around the administrative annihilation from Phillip Glass: a corrupt decision to create detriment and inconvenience, and in breach of my civil liberties as a 40-year customer of the Commonwealth Bank.

A bank that refuses to provide the response to my report of over 100 ABNs on the ABN Lookup site linked to my totally unique name, Barran Resonance Dodger. Even the Goulbourn bank manager told me his hands are tied and he too cannot access it. I barely raised my voice to insist on a response to what could be the biggest character assassination and systemic corruption and civil death case in the democracies’ history, when they blamed me for being aggressive, as a DARVO technique of psychological warfare and blaming the victim to evade accountability, and I was told if I didn’t leave, police would be called.

Sixteen times, for not having a home

Hospitals are ineffective at treating me. I’ve been incarcerated under the Mental Health Act sixteen times in about five years, always for not having a home, and as the victim of political targeting and coercive financial control and deliberate sabotage of my prosperity, and a categorical denial of legal aid, which is an absurd paradoxical inversion of the very reason legal aid exists.

My first discharge summary by Dr Lagrasso cited adjustment disorder as the principal diagnosis, with specific mention of no psychosis and no delusion. Adjustment disorder is a diagnosis referring to a delayed response to abuse, or to actual verifiable stressors, as a delayed but very human response to trauma. I had just survived a “fatal” suicide attempt in which I was discovered by accident, and call it fate, God or the universe, I was revived from death. My tragedy left me with a cognitive brain injury, yet no compensation was offered, and my tragedy was whitewashed by every carer, hospital, my mental health worker, and also my blood family, whose betrayal of me was not as clear as now.

After they created conditions in which I was not expected to survive, they refused to protest my exile, poverty, financial abuse, targeting and asylum claim. I was living in my car with my therapy dog as they slept guilt-free in million-dollar houses nearby. And after all that, they worked with police to identify me as the risk. My mother, April, sobbed a legal AVO guaranteeing zero contact. When the NDIS minister, Shorten, also identified me as a risk to him, he weaponised his enormous power and privilege, conspiring with Legal Aid, Victoria Police, the hospital and the Magistrates’ Court to force me to a court date without legal aid, which then forced me to exile.

No NDIS support worker anywhere, including Cass Mackay, has ever acknowledged that as a criminal way to target a vulnerable person, while my carers benefit financially from entrapping me in poverty and participating in a legal-aid ban and total police complicity, and a well-funded total media ban across every radio and television station I had previously given my time to appear on.

Why I wrote that to Phillip

I was so angry with Phillip Glass, who I say was appointed to align with what I call the criminal mastermind Shorten, and with Iasonidis and Ridley, that I wrote him an email and called him a “Godless cunt”. I say he maliciously targeted a vulnerable person he was paid to care for, but in reality blocked my legal aid, removed more and more of my pension, and blocked access to my Commonwealth Bank internet banking and app. Financial benefit for him from causing harm to a vulnerable person in his care is not, in my account, just bureaucratic oversight. I say it is deliberate and malicious political targeting, and I have never met human harm like it.

I say he was pre-warned and told how to treat me when my name came across his desk, and that he was expected to obey it because he is paid a blood-money wage by the government that politically targeted me, exiled me, attempted to assassinate me and erase me, and is still trying to cover it up. That is my account. It does not disappear because no institution has acknowledged the appointment or the offence. The email, who it was sent to, and the words, are the letter to Phillip. What his office wrote about the $50 cut is on the Able Point email.

Phillip would expect workers’ compensation if he got injured at work. He would expect compensation if he died in the hospital. If it was his home, and his position, removed, he would expect compensation. If a violent and maliciously narcissistic form of fiancé controlled his life with impunity, he would expect compensation for that. But he knows all of this with me, and he actively aligned with the perpetrators and the criminals. Meaning: when you are aligned with criminals, and you don’t call out the corruption and the fraud, then you yourself are a criminal, and also a fraud. That is what I say about him. I am not asking a court for permission to have said it.

Sukhi Tear, in Western Australia

Sukhi Tear is my support coordinator, all the way in Western Australia, at the NDIS provider organisation I call criminal, Diversitas, diversitaswa.com. I have never met her. She refuses a legal cease and desist. I have been reported as a missing person under her support coordination a number of times, across three states. I say that reveals not neutral bureaucratic oversight but a deliberate campaign, in which she financially benefited from targeting me and exiling me, forcing me from the only safe place I was entrapped in.

I say she also worked with Salman Kazmi to approve the finances that he siphoned from my account, illegally, in order to illegally buy me a car. It was framed as my benefit. It is now, to me, a means by which I was to escape the highly surveilled, V2K-saturated torture chamber I suffered inside, as a literal torture victim. She approved it, I say, to force an escape route that left me unprotected and vulnerable to paid vigilantes she refuses to deny exist.

It is almost as if she knew I was going to be erased by Houd Meraby, and that she was appointed, in a role located in another state, as a key stakeholder in coordinating my harm and entrapping me in poverty and torture, by the same NDIS minister, Bill Shorten. I say she oversaw the financing of my escape in a dud car, consciously, and delivered me into the arms of criminal assassins she was well aware of. I say she knew of the attempted assassination because I proved it to her with Ben’s evidence, and she refused to disprove it as false, or inaccurate, or the symptom of any mental illness.

Any wonder, I say, that she was deliberately selected from another state, and that her profession is linked to the court and the criminal justice system, which is very evident, and that they would have known that when he appointed her to act in a coordinated way, designed to physically remove me from the existence of this universe. That is my account of her significance. Her silence does not erase the assassination, and it does not erase the account of her appointment. The cease and desist, and the links, are Sukhi Tear.

They refused, and I name them

All Able Point staff refused to help me: Cass Kim, Alyssie, Danny, Ben, and Brett in management, and Rachel the CEO, who refuses to provide me with her verified government ID. This is not only unethical, because it reveals the clear avoiding of accountability in breach of the ethics her position of privilege demands of her, but in breach of the ethical infrastructure of holding a CEO position. The ABN of Able Point she is linked to has changed its legal name numerous times and operates under over two ABNs of Sahara Disability Services, located at several addresses. Able Point has no official bricks-and-mortar office. I say it operates as a ghost enterprise to avoid accountability, and to avoid the obligations of being paid to financially benefit from entrapping a gay, disabled, unprotected whistleblower.

I have been in political exile and forced from Victoria, in what I say was a coordinated, covert, deliberate conspiracy to force me under conditions of political exile, in breach of civil and political rights. I was then forced from safe housing to further exile, in a car bought for me by Salman Kazmi, another NDIS provider, who acted as if to favour me and my mobility, but who I say was aligned with Bill Shorten. I say Shorten not only breached his ethical obligations of caring for a disabled person under his ministry, but actively coordinated my exile and forced homelessness.

I allege I survived an assassination attempt after a criminal pretend NDIS provider, Houd Meraby, flew from Melbourne to Sydney. I say Tony Ridley confessed to me, in what I describe as a honey-pot entrapment, that Bill was exposed for being aware of six billion in misappropriated funds at the NDIS, which he was aware of but refused to call out. I was forced again to exile after a stranger, who otherwise would never have known, warned me that Houd was sent to erase me. I had initially accepted his help.

Ben, another NDIS provider, confirmed to me what I call the conspiracy to murder. I demanded he send the evidence to the Federal Police. He thought I was initially crazy, but he did, and then he changed his mind about me being delusional after he said police confirmed the murder and assassination attempt was a “close call”. I say the authorities did not save my life because they cared. They intervened because they were forced to, as I had published evidence, and Bill Shorten would have been exposed as ordering the hit. That is my allegation. It stands on this page. No institution’s silence erases it.

Ben also confirmed, as I understood him, that a woman I had sex with, at a police break-up in Victoria in my very young life, was paid to fabricate a report and a false allegation because she wanted to destroy my life, and that this was cooperated with by a homophobic police family. Years later he related this false allegation, which he said was confirmed by the Federal Police. No crime happened. The only crime, in my account, was a gay humiliation ritual, a coordinated attack by police, family and committee members, who said to him it was the reason the police have treated me so badly over my life, and why I have never been able to report a crime.

Ben was forced to sign an NDA, an agency-grade signing of a document, and it disappeared off his phone after he signed it. He said to me that I have exposed corruption that goes all the way to the top, and that he was scared they could put a hit on him too. The evidence of the text messages from Ben, over months, is published under these words, publicly and permanently.

I say I am the victim of an assassination plot ultimately ordered by the same minister who exiled me, in a conspiracy with the legal fraternity, the hospital system and the Magistrates’ Court, and that I have been a targeted individual for over thirty-five years. I say this is detailed in over thirty-five years of official government documents, from across agencies, politicians, services and departments, with all of them denying legal aid, and with decisions I say were made to cause my detriment. I say that slowly deconstructed my civil liberties, my human rights and my legal rights, and desecrated my spirit, my experience, my story and my person, because I exist and I have that evidence. In my view I am an existential threat to the cover they kept.

I have been forced to exile, which no one will even mention or call out. I say that is because these NDIS companies are corrupt to the bone, and the support workers are controlled by being paid their wage as blood money from the NDIS. They are paid their wage to guarantee complicity in aligning with Shorten, who I say still has control over police and the legal fraternity and commands the media, and silenced me with the AVO, which made mention of my name forbidden. I say this is evidence of a designed silencing campaign, to guarantee no media would report on me as an internally displaced person and asylum seeker inside the same democracy as my citizenship.

I say that is the most significant criminal corruption case, at the highest level, in Australian living memory, targeting a single vulnerable, brain-injured, disabled, gay, unprotected whistleblower, without legal help: institutionally complicit police, accused without charge, libel and slander in a way that guaranteed I would suffer as if I had committed a crime, when the only crime was the corruption of the people who did it. I should have been able to report my targeting. I say there was institutional complicity, and a refusal, across politicians, the National Anti-Corruption Commission, the Ombudsman, the Prime Minister who I say gaslit my entire complaint, and every agency over thirty-five years. That record is preserved on this website. You can look at it yourself.

I say this is evidence of a state-sanctioned targeted killing of a gay, disabled, unprotected whistleblower. I say it is the biggest criminal organisation and murder-corruption case in Australia’s living memory to do with a single vulnerable person who never did anything wrong except speak the truth, prove it with facts, and exist. Not a single professional in the entire country, in my experience, has had the courage to look me in the eye and disprove it with a fact. Their account, as I have met it, is slander and whispers, used as a mechanism to guarantee that no one would help me.

In order to sustain the corruption and trap me in abuse, neglect, poverty, financial abuse, administrative annihilation, legal and media bans, police corruption, and the denial of oversight and anti-corruption bodies, libel and slander are used as a technique. There has been no arrest, no charge, and no legal process against me on that report. That is significant. This was a coordinated, long-term political, bureaucratic, economic, financial and legal targeting, and the only thing I ever did was speak the truth, with honesty, and live unapologetically my authentic life. Most people who have given their consent to be ruled by a corrupt government are, I believe, envious and jealous that I have done so, and survived, and lived, and rebuilt myself from the ashes of the destruction the government left me in, deliberately, with no help from any cop, any lawyer, or any politician. I rebuilt from the ashes of my soul. I looked into my darkness. I made peace, and I healed. I am happy. I have the truth. I know it is real. I know it is true.

The Witness Remains · 29 October 2026

I am alive, and I am asking a person to help me reach safety.

My name is Dr Richard William McLean. I am known as Barran Dodger. The dog beside me is Crystal. I am alive. I am at 55 Archbold Road, Long Jetty NSW. My phone is 0431 300 940, and that number is also my PayID. I need medicine, access to my own bank account, and transport. I am asking for a person, not another referral.

I have carried this for thirty-five years. The asking was answered with letters, referrals and silence, while my safety, my money, my medicine and my dog were left in other people’s hands. The master address to the Prime Minister is on its own page, in my words. The films come next, because a voice is easier to meet than a file. A film does not, by itself, prove an allegation.

The documents are public. A letter proves what that letter says. The testimony is marked as testimony. Preservation first. Examination second. Conclusion last. Nothing below has been removed: not a word, not a link, not a picture.

Main SOS · His words · 3 November 2026 · Testimony. It is not conditional upon legal representation

Bill Shorten commands and expects every single human to betray me.

Bill Shorten commands and expects every single human to betray me, and somehow he has done this. I published an SOS to escape what I describe as an entrapment here with Ablepoint, an NDIS provider I say is corrupt. I am still entrapped by Ablepoint. I have published, publicly, that I wish to leave, because I say I am being abused, neglected, surveilled and tortured, and I am in poverty.

Bill Shorten is my abuser. I allege that he sent Hood Mirabi, a person I describe as a criminal and not an NDIS provider, supposedly to help me. After I had spoken to Hood, I was told, anonymously, by a person who would otherwise have had no reason to know that I had spoken to him, that Hood would, quote unquote, “erase me.”

I allege that Shorten ordered police to arrest me on a false charge. A magistrate ordered an AVO against me, as if I was the threat and I was the criminal, when I say it was Shorten who was entrapping me and abusing me. I question the relationship between my petitions concerning Bill Shorten, the petition that carries an identification number, and the timing of his failure to become Prime Minister, when Anthony Albanese did. I ask that this connection be looked into. I do not ask that the question be treated as the answer.

I say Shorten has betrayed his ethics, weaponising his immense power to force me into political exile. I was not expected to survive in the ashes of total human betrayal, living in my car in New South Wales with my therapy dog. I have been character-assassinated and falsely accused. I say I hear the accusations through the libel and slander of audio harassment.

I have a PhD. I am more educated than Shorten. A PhD means do no harm. I say Bill Shorten has caused me immense harm and suffering. He has the command of police, the media, the courts and the NDIS. I say I was falsely accused of sexual crimes which Federal Police confirmed were consensual. I say Bill paid his way out of his own allegations. My evidence is published, with facts and evidence, online, for everyone to see. His evidence is not.

He was legally obliged to support me as the Minister overseeing the NDIS. I say he caused deliberate harm and, I allege, actually ordered my assassination. I have integrity and he does not. I have honesty and truth. I say he is deceptive, a liar, and corrupt. He has immense political power that I say is corrupt. I have published truth with integrity. I say he declared me a threat to him as he abused me, and then, I allege, ordered my assassination.

Ben, my NDIS contact, confirmed the attempt from Federal Police as a, quote unquote, “close call.” I want the detail of his texts on this record. Those texts describe his whole situation, and the non-disclosure agreement which I say Ben was forced to sign by the Secret Service or the Federal Police who intercepted the assassination attempt. The messages, as sent, are Ben’s texts. What Ben wrote is not yet the police occurrence.

I say it costs taxpayers a black budget of immense money to guarantee complicity with every cop, lawyer, politician, and all of my family and friends, and for Ablepoint, which I say is corrupt, to benefit financially from my entrapment. I say this massive black budget of taxpayer money is paid by the government as Bill Shorten’s own personal vendetta against a person he should have cared for, and that he has attempted to erase me physically from this existence.

I am alive. I healed without the help of any lawyer, any cop, any politician, or anyone. I say he is terrified, because my mere existence shines a light on what I call his ultimate evil corruption. This is my testimony. I am not asking permission to have experienced it. The original records remain, and they are to be read.

3 November 2026 · Added to the SOS · Testimony · It stands without a lawyer’s permission

I want to leave, but I have been deprived of the means to escape.

I want to leave. I have repeatedly made that clear. But I have no car, no practical means of transport, and no financial capacity to recover the vehicle that was taken from me. The Public Guardian took my car, and I have been left without the very means I need to escape the circumstances in which I find myself.

The history of how I became trapped is important because it demonstrates the cumulative absurdity of what has happened. When I was hospitalised, I had no home through no fault of my own. I had been forced out of my home and made vulnerable. Instead of addressing the circumstances that had deprived me of housing, financial security and independence, the system placed me in hospital. I did not need to be deprived of my freedom and treated as though hospitalisation was the answer to my circumstances. I needed my compensation, financial security, a lawyer and assistance to restore my independence.

At one point, a car was purchased for me so that I could escape the circumstances in which I was living. Yet I was subsequently hospitalised, and my circumstances deteriorated further. The absurdity is that my Centrelink Disability Support Pension card carried the hospital as my residential address. Consider what that means: a person who had lost his home, been deprived of his financial independence and had his circumstances converted into a hospital admission was effectively recorded as residing in an institution rather than being assisted to recover a home and a life.

I needed a home. I needed my money. I needed legal representation. I needed the means to participate in society and defend my rights. Instead, I experienced a sequence of decisions that removed the practical means of achieving those things.

The financial consequences were particularly cruel. While I was in hospital, the financial guardianship arrangements required me to pay rent out of my pension. The very income that should have been protected and administered in my interests was being used to meet housing expenses while I was unable to occupy my home in the ordinary way. I ask what meaningful protection was being provided by a financial guardianship arrangement if its practical effect was to leave me without control over my money while continuing to impose financial obligations upon me. Guardianship is supposed to protect a person’s interests, not compound their vulnerability or make it harder for them to regain independence.

The injustice becomes even more significant when considered alongside the removal of my vehicle. The police permitted the government repossession of my car, as I understand the events, without any compensation being provided to me for the loss. I dispute the legitimacy and fairness of what occurred and demand an explanation of the authority under which the vehicle was taken, the process followed, the legal basis for the decision and the arrangements for compensation or return. I have no practical means of getting my car back, and no vehicle with which to leave.

This is not a minor inconvenience. It is a direct restriction on my ability to exercise freedom of movement, secure alternative accommodation, access services, obtain legal assistance and remove myself from an environment in which I have repeatedly said I do not wish to remain.

The financial guardianship arrangements and the Public Guardian’s involvement must also be examined alongside the restrictions I have encountered in accessing Legal Aid. The Public Guardian has not resolved the legal assistance problem. Instead, the existing Legal Aid restriction has been accepted as another administrative reality. The result, as I experience it, is inter-agency entrapment: one institution makes or maintains a decision, another accepts its consequences, and neither takes responsibility for ensuring that I have a practical way to challenge the decisions affecting my life.

This is how accountability disappears between agencies. Each institution can point to another institution’s decision, policy, authority or responsibility. Meanwhile, I am the person who lives with the consequences. I am left without my vehicle, without the financial independence necessary to organise my departure, and without the legal representation necessary to challenge the arrangements that have contributed to my circumstances.

I cannot reasonably be expected to possess the financial resources, physical capacity, transport, institutional access or legal assistance necessary to escape a situation when the very systems that are supposed to protect me have become part of the barriers I must overcome. It is an extraordinary contradiction to tell a person that they have rights while leaving them without the practical means to exercise those rights.

When you remove someone’s money, you do more than remove a financial resource. You remove the means by which that person participates in a democracy. Money is necessary to secure accommodation, obtain transport, engage a lawyer, purchase necessities and make independent decisions. When a person is deprived of those means, their formal rights can remain written on paper while their practical ability to exercise them is systematically diminished.

That is the significance of what I describe as the systemic removal of the attributes necessary to live with dignity in a democracy. Income, compensation, money, a home, safety, employment, human rights and legal rights are not isolated conveniences. They are interconnected conditions of independent existence. Remove enough of them, and a person can become trapped in circumstances from which they cannot reasonably escape.

Every person deserves a home. I have repeatedly sought the opportunity to leave and establish an independent life. Yet I feel as though I have been given a prison made not of walls and bars, but of bureaucracy, financial restrictions, institutional decisions, unanswered correspondence and agencies that refuse to take responsibility. I am not a criminal. I should not have to live as though I am serving a sentence imposed by an accumulation of administrative decisions that I cannot afford to challenge.

I also raise the significance of Ablepoint’s alleged refusal to report or appropriately escalate a death threat before the Wyong Court proceedings. This is a matter requiring a proper examination of the original communications, the circumstances in which the threat was raised, who received the information, what action was requested, what action was taken and why. If a provider entrusted with supporting and safeguarding a vulnerable participant receives information about a death threat, the response cannot simply be silence or administrative avoidance. The question of whether the threat was appropriately reported and escalated is fundamental to the assessment of safeguarding, duty of care and institutional accountability.

I have repeatedly raised concerns about my safety and circumstances. I have sought assistance. I have asked to leave. I have asked for responses. I have asked for accountability. Yet the CEO has refused to respond to me or provide a substantive answer to the concerns I have raised. Instead, I have encountered silence. That silence has become part of the problem because it prevents meaningful examination of the decisions, allegations and failures I am asking the organisations involved to address.

The financial guardianship arrangements are supposed to protect me. The disability support system is supposed to support me. The provider is supposed to safeguard me. The legal system is supposed to provide access to justice. The police are supposed to protect the public. Government agencies are supposed to administer their responsibilities lawfully and accountably. But what happens when the person who needs those protections experiences each institution as another obstacle to obtaining them?

I allege that the cumulative effect of these decisions and omissions has been to silence me as a whistleblower, undermine my independence and erase my ability to participate in ordinary life. I use the word erasure deliberately. A person does not have to be physically killed to experience the destruction of their public identity, economic independence, security, relationships and capacity to be heard. When a person’s body remains alive but the practical conditions necessary to live with dignity are systematically removed, the human consequences can be devastating.

I regard this as an issue of profound public importance. The allegations must be investigated through the original records, correspondence, financial transactions, guardianship decisions, police records, hospital records, Legal Aid decisions and provider communications. It is not sufficient for each agency to defend its own isolated decision without examining the cumulative consequences of those decisions.

I want to leave. I have said so. I have no car. I have no practical means of recovering the car that was taken. I have not received compensation for its loss. I have experienced restrictions on my access to money and legal assistance. I am asking for the means to regain my independence, not another explanation of why somebody else is responsible.

This is the corruption I am asking the public to examine: not merely an individual decision, but the possibility that multiple institutions can contribute to a person’s entrapment while each escapes responsibility for the overall outcome.

I will not accept silence as an adequate response. I will not accept the proposition that my circumstances are simply somebody else’s administrative problem. I will not accept being deprived of the means to leave and then being held responsible for remaining where I am.

They attempted to silence me. They attempted to erase me. But they failed to stop me from documenting what happened, preserving the evidence and asking the questions that remain unanswered.

The silence has broken. I am still here. And I want my freedom, my independence, my vehicle, my financial security, access to legal representation and the opportunity to live in a home of my own.

I am asking for assistance to leave, and I am asking for an independent investigation into how I came to be deprived of the means to do so.

One important distinction: I have framed the alleged unlawful repossession, institutional failures and deliberate silencing as allegations requiring investigation, rather than presenting disputed criminal conduct as already judicially established. That makes the SOS more defensible and harder to dismiss.

The Complete Literary, Spiritual, Prophetic, Political and Forensic Works of Barran Dodger

Master forensic publishing catalogue — restarted from the beginning

(Unfinished)

I will treat this as a new catalogue, beginning at Catalogue No. 001. The earlier numbering is not being carried forward, so the register will have one clean sequence and no inherited gaps.
The central rule is that a title is not automatically a verified, distinct publication. I will record the exact title and source path, distinguish a separate work from a duplicate copy or alternate edition, and mark information that cannot be established. The catalogue will cover the main repository first, then relevant companion repositories and linked archive material. The spreadsheet register is part of the deliverable, alongside the written catalogue.
Repository discovery — account-wide starting point
The GitHub account search identifies 10 public repositories under wezzo72:

golden-witness-publishing-house⁠.
-church-of-barran-dodger⁠.

barran-dodger-archive⁠.

barrandodger-rebuild⁠.
barran-dodger-ministry⁠.
Correction to the count: the search returned 11 repositories, not 10. I have listed all 11 results. This is the initial account discovery, not yet proof that every repository has been exhaustively examined.

Spreadsheet register — initial rows
The spreadsheet should retain all 24 forensic fields specified in your commission. The following is a copyable CSV starter for the first 20 entries, using the verified bibliographic fields available so far. It is not yet the completed spreadsheet or the complete inventory.
Provenance and completeness notes at this stage
The pdf-archive register identifies the 59 PDFs by PDF CreationDate, filename, size and page count. These are useful metadata fields, but they do not independently establish the date of composition, first publication or copyright ownership.
The three Eliven Chain files dated 25 September 2025 have matching recorded size and page count. They remain separate file records, but their distinct-work status is unresolved.
The two Eliven Chain Has Been Summoned records have materially different page counts (52 and 350). They require full comparison to determine whether the latter is an expanded edition.
A PDF may contain multiple separately titled essays. Those constituent works will be added as their own catalogue entries only when their titles and boundaries can be verified from the PDF.
The 451-line website-page-pdfs/index.csv is a separate index of archived website-page PDFs. It is a discovery source, not proof that all 451 rows are independent literary works.
The main repository’s spiritual index and the PDF archive are separate sources. Their overlapping titles must be reconciled before portfolio totals are calculated.
Next catalogue sequence: No. 021 onward, beginning with the remaining Gospel/prophetic PDFs and then moving into the political, forensic, creative and autobiographical divisions. The final counts remain deliberately unreported until the title and edition reconciliation is complete.

Provenance and reconciliation notes from this section
The source README also identifies five additional Gospel/prophetic PDFs outside the 59-file dated ZIP register:
2026-05-01__My_Boaz_Is_Coming.pdf
2026-05-10__a-divine-reckoning-to-those-who-chose-this-dr-richard-mclean.pdf
2026-05-09__forensic-analysis-57-prophetic-declaration.pdf
2026-05-09__forensic-analysis-58-prophetic-fck-you-declaration.pdf
2026-05-01__my-boaz-is-coming.pdf
The two My Boaz Is Coming filenames are a potential duplicate or edition pair. They must remain separate source records until their contents and hashes are compared.
The README also lists numerous files under “Skipped stubs” because they are smaller than 50 KB. These are not silently discarded from the overall audit: they belong in a separate placeholder/stub register, not automatically in the publication catalogue as complete works.
The archive README further identifies later shelf publications, including The Crowned Witness — Barran Dodger and the Indictment of Nations, The Prophecy of the Witness, The Prophecy of the One They Tried to Erase, and They Could Not Erase What God Preserved. These need reconciliation against the main repository and the existing numbered records before being assigned new catalogue numbers.
Progress: Catalogue Nos. 001–040 have now been carried forward in sequence. The catalogue is not complete: the remaining dated PDF records, the five extra PDFs, the later significant-shelf works, and the wider repositories still need to be examined. I will continue from No. 041.

Status of the first dated PDF register
59 of 59 indexed entries catalogued
The first 59 dated entries in gospel-prophetic-papers/README.md have now been assigned catalogue numbers 001–059. This means that the dated index has been transcribed into the catalogue sequence; it does not mean that all 59 full texts have been read, that all editions have been reconciled, or that the entire publishing archive has been inventoried.
The same README identifies additional material that must be included in the wider audit:
	•	Five extra PDFs outside the 59-file release ZIPs, including the two similarly named My Boaz Is Coming files.
	•	Four later significant-shelf works, including The Crowned Witness, The Prophecy of the Witness, The Prophecy of the One They Tried to Erase, and They Could Not Erase What God Preserved.
	•	The Gospel of Exposing Darkness, dated 21 October 2026, with a recorded SHA-256 hash.
	•	A separate group of small placeholder PDFs that must be distinguished from complete publications.
Next sequence: I will reconcile these additional works against the main Barrandodger repository’s spiritual-prophetic index and the separate Witness Remains catalogue, then continue into the other publication categories. The numbering will continue from No. 060; confirmed duplicates will be cross-referenced rather than silently counted as new works.

Important correction to the catalogue method
The spiritual-prophetic-index.html file contains 32 indexed cards, while the separate The Witness Remains — Documentary Record, Gospels and Prophetic Narratives page describes ten foundational Gospel works and ten prophetic narratives. These are different kinds of indexes, and their counts should not be added together as if they were separate publications.
The spiritual-prophetic index itself expressly cautions that these works are spiritual, prophetic and philosophical testimony, not court findings. That is the appropriate distinction to preserve in the catalogue.
The archive also has a separate Gospel of the Witness Who Remained manuscript. Its text refers to a number of other works and describes the Enliven/Eliven/Aliven Chain as a connected collection. Those named works will need individual source verification before being added as distinct catalogue entries.
Current sequence: Nos. 001–069 have been assigned. The next task is to extract and reconcile the complete 31-work significant shelf, the ten foundational Gospel titles and ten prophetic narratives, and the other named manuscripts and publication pages. I will continue from No. 070, while keeping duplicate editions and HTML/PDF copies cross-referenced rather than inflating the number of distinct works.

Master publishing catalogue — Part 5
Catalogue Nos. 070–075: Foundational Gospel and prophetic works
This section moves beyond the PDF release index and examines the separate The Witness Remains catalogue against actual files in the main GitHub repository. An important distinction emerges: some titles have an identifiable manuscript or published page, while others appear only as named works in an editorial index. I am recording that distinction rather than treating every index entry as a verified, separately published book.
The source index itself says that its twenty foundational and prophetic titles are a curated navigation list, and that some may be related editions, collection titles or foundational works rather than separate canonical works. Source: The Witness Remains — Documentary Record, Gospels and Prophetic Narratives⁠.

	•	Additional complete text: The Documentary Trap — Complete Plain Text⁠.
	•	Format and length: Multiple plain-text and HTML presentations; total page or word count not established in this catalogue entry.
	•	Description: The work develops the idea that the institutions involved in an individual’s complaints and dealings generate their own documentary record. It argues that the resulting letters, decisions, acknowledgements, referrals, omissions and dates can be examined independently of the author’s recollection. The original text explicitly cautions that the mere existence of official records does not, by itself, prove a coordinated plan or establish wrongdoing.
	•	Themes: Documentary evidence, institutional decision-making, administrative records, chronology, accountability, credibility and independent examination.
	•	Intellectual contribution: The central contribution is a method of shifting attention from accepting or rejecting a person’s account to examining what contemporaneous institutional documents actually establish. It also calls for separating documentary fact, allegation, inference and legal conclusion.
	•	Relationship to other publications: Central to The Witness Remains, The Digital Apocalypse of Administrative Annihilation, The Cost of Erasure and the master evidence register.
	•	Publication unit: One principal work with multiple clean-text, complete-text and HTML presentations.
	•	Status: Multiple source files located in the main repository.
	•	Potential format: A standalone documentary-method book or report, with a source-linked chronology and an appendix explaining evidentiary categories.
	•	Recommended division: Documentary Method, Forensic Commentary and Institutional Accountability.
	•	Evidence: Original manuscript, final clean-text file and complete plain-text file. The original begins by explaining the documentary paradox and expressly distinguishes the author’s interpretation from what the records themselves prove.
	•	Duplication/version relationship: The repository contains at least three text files with overlapping content, as well as HTML presentations. They should be treated as versions of one principal work until a textual comparison establishes distinct substantive editions.

Catalogue progress and next sequence
Current assigned sequence: 001–088.
The next section will examine the archive’s other political, legal, forensic and autobiographical titles, including the personal statement of exile, the documentary chronology, the numbered forensic analyses, and the publications that appear in the archive’s book and academic indexes.
One important bibliographic distinction is now clear: the repository’s own books.html separates independently reviewed or publisher-listed works from titles that remain in its research queue. I will preserve that distinction in the final catalogue rather than presenting every title as independently verified.

Catalogue sequence: 109.
A useful distinction is now emerging in the register: the archive contains original literary works, expanded or alternate editions, and companion analytical documents. Those need separate classifications. Otherwise, a single essay appearing as HTML, PDF, a timestamped copy and a video analysis could be incorrectly counted as four books.

This is important: an index card, a website tab, a PDF and a revised edition are not necessarily four independent literary works. The final register needs to preserve each format and provenance record while avoiding inflated publication totals.
Next, the catalogue needs to move beyond the spiritual index and systematically examine the remaining public repositories and the archive’s book, testimony, essays, ministry and publishing-house sections.


Newly identified repository material
The next repository pass has uncovered a substantial additional collection that was not represented in the earlier 129-entry sequence.
The wezzo72/barran-dodger-archive repository currently exposes 18,422 file entries in its recursive Git tree. That is a mixed archive of publications, source records, attachments, code and supporting material—not 18,422 separate books. The separate wezzo72/-church-of-barran-dodger repository exposes 108 files, including several distinct Gospel, testimony and literary PDFs. The publishing-house repository also has its own catalogue and inventory reports.
This changes the scope of the final completeness assessment: the earlier sequence is a developing publication catalogue, not yet a complete inventory of all repositories. The original repositories remain untouched. wezzo72/Barrandodger: Web · GitHub

Catalogue sequence: 140.
The next repository pass should prioritise the publishing-house inventory files, particularly duplicate-and-version-report.md, missing-and-inaccessible-materials.md and publication-readiness-report.md. These are likely to help reconcile the growing catalogue and prevent multiple copies of the same work being counted as independent books.
The main archive repository also contains many more named PDFs than the church collection. I will keep separating original literary works from letters, affidavits, agency records, evidence exhibits, timestamp receipts and other ancillary materials rather than inflating the publication count.







Publishing-house inventory findings
The publishing-house reports add an important qualification to the catalogue:
	•	The scan dated 19 October 2026 reports 8,113 deduplicated public publication files across PDF, HTML, Markdown, text, EPUB, DOCX and RTF formats, after excluding dependency folders and pointer-sized files.
	•	The scan covers 11 relevant repositories, including the main website, church, rebuild, PDF archive, Backup, Myaidrive and the large archive repository.
	•	It explicitly says that thousands of PDFs and HTML captures were enumerated but not fully downloaded or read. Therefore, the 8,113 figure is a file inventory, not a verified count of 8,113 distinct literary works.
	•	It identifies 4,959 pointer-or-stub blobs. Those cannot be treated as readable editions.
	•	It also records several sought titles that were not located in that scan, including Recovered, Not Cured, Evidence 100 and Final Immortalization Statement.
Sources: Duplicate and version report⁠ · Missing and inaccessible materials⁠ · Publication readiness report⁠.
The next entries below are additional distinct titles found in the large archive’s PDF collection. They are catalogued as identified publications, not as fully read or authenticated works.

Important provenance qualification

Next continuation
The next group should examine The Gospel According to Barran Dodger, The Chronicles of the New Earth against its actual larger PDF, and the other spiritual and documentary titles that have substantial files rather than pointer-sized entries.
A further distinction is now important for the final register: a title record, a file manifestation, a readable source document and a verified edition are four different things. The catalogue will preserve all four statuses so that the number of archived files is never mistaken for the number of original works.

By

Two messages, on every page. 11 November 2026. To his mother, by iMessage, before the assassination was written up: “whistleblower, in fact it goes to the top. The office of prime minister and cabinet refused my FOI: that's because of the cover up and how I was framed. I don't want to worry you... but they will kill me. By omission and the violence of neglect. In fact I was already murdered, but you said I got over that. It was deemed a lethal attempt in hospital FOI.” He then demanded that Ben give the evidence to the Federal Police. The SMS with Ben Ndis Help, as read: “I feel like leaving because I know they will stitch me up as the mad one. The police want to know if you are mentally ready to challenge Bill Shorten in a court of law as his lawyers might use your history of mental health as an excuse to discredit your story. Yes. But I know they were doing that already. That's why I absconded. He's weaponised the” — the last line is cut off on the screen. Both messages, in the words of the screens.

On every page. Institutional silence is not evidentiary rebuttal. Lack of official recognition is not proof of non-existence. The dignity of a human being is not conferred by government authority. The same law and the same files apply to the whole site. The law and the files. An NDIS provider may not abuse, neglect or stalk a participant. The V2K papers. NDIS Act, Code of Conduct, reportable incidents, public-interest disclosures. Ben’s texts. Ben-NDIS-Help. Ablepoint request. What is fixed.
